    文献研究：PhysECD: A Physics-Constrained E(3)-Equivariant Framework for Electronic Circular Dichroism Spectrum Prediction#
    PhysECD: A Physics-Constrained E(3)-Equivariant Framework for Electronic Circular Dichroism Spectrum Prediction#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Yi Jiang, Letian Chen, Runhan Shi, Liangzhaoxuan Han, Tong Zhu, Yang Yang🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    3
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21892v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The electronic circular dichroism (ECD) spectrum is a primary experimental probe for assigning the absolute configuration of chiral molecules, yet interpreting a measured spectrum requires time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) calculations that can cost hours per molecule and must be repeated for every candidate stereoisomer and conformation. We present PhysECD, a physics-constrained, parity-aware E(3)-equivariant framework that bypasses computationally expensive TDDFT and predicts ECD spectra directly from the 3D structure of an individual conformer. Instead of regressing the spectrum as an opaque sequence, PhysECD predicts the physical quantities that generate it: per-state excitation energies and electric and magnetic transition dipoles. These quantities determine the rotatory strength R -- the dot product of the two dipoles, a pseudoscalar that reverses sign under mirror reflection -- and yield the final spectrum through a differentiable Gaussian-broadening formula derived from the underlying physics. The parity structure of the equivariant features guarantees the correct chiroptical symmetry: reflecting a molecule exactly negates the predicted spectrum. On the CMCDS dataset, PhysECD attains a per-molecule spectral Pearson correlation of 0.642 (mean) / 0.822 (median), substantially exceeding prior learned predictors while remaining physically interpretable. Experiments across multiple backbones further show that the framework is backbone-agnostic, paving the way for real-time assignment of absolute configuration.

    利用机器学习力场研究高压自由基聚合过程中聚乙烯的第一性原理原子结构与动力学#
    First-Principles Atomistic Structure and Dynamics of Polyethylene During High-Pressure Radical Polymerization via Machine Learning Force Fields#
    🧭 化学 / 分子P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Bharatha K. Gunawardana, Teresa Shah, Bicha Azizova, Deepa Ranabhat, Yizhi Song, Akshath Shastri, Srinjoy Ghose, Thomas E. Gartner III, Hsin-Yu Ko🔥 AI 100
    相关度
    1

    📄 摘要：聚乙烯（PE）是最常用的合成聚合物之一。尽管 PE 的合成和加工工艺已经十分成熟，但在原子尺度上精确确定其微观结构，即每个原子的位置，仍主要局限于高度晶化体系。人们通常通过使用经验原子间势的计算机模拟来弥补这一缺口；经验势以近似但高效的方式描述原子间相互作用，从而达到描述大分子所需的长度和时间尺度。这些经验势通常能够较好地描述体相性质和/或集体性质，但在复杂体系中会面临化学真实性方面的挑战，例如反应过程中的体系。在本工作中，我们将深度势（DP）机器学习力场的计算效率，与由 SeA 高通量框架实现的、经第一性原理范德华修正的杂化密度泛函理论（DFT）的化学真实性相结合，以应对这一挑战。利用该方法，我们研究了常见高压，即超临界，自由基聚合条件下乙烯溶剂中的 PE 低聚物和聚合物的结构与动力学。我们发现，当链长大于约 n ≃ 6 时，含自由基 PE 低聚物的局部溶剂化环境趋于收敛，这表明在低聚物上训练的机器学习力场可以扩展到明显更长的聚合物。随后，我们通过表征单链结构与动力学的分子量标度关系，进一步确认这些模型能够扩展到长 PE 链，并发现其表现出经典的良溶剂行为。我们的 PE 机器学习力场在广泛的热力学状态点和链长范围内、以完整原子分辨率保持了稳定且一致的精度，从而为基于第一性原理的聚合物结构与性质预测铺平了道路。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21741v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Polyethylene (PE) is one of the most commonly used synthetic polymers. While the synthesis and processing protocols for PE are well established, precise experimental assignment of microscopic structures at atomistic resolution (i.e., the position of each atom) remains largely limited to highly crystalline systems. This gap is often addressed via computer simulations using empirical interatomic potentials, which use approximate but efficient descriptions of interatomic interactions to reach the length and time scales needed to describe macromolecules. These empirical potentials typically perform well for bulk and/or collective properties but face challenges with chemical realism for complex systems, e.g., during reactive processes. In this work, we address this challenge by combining the computational efficiency of a deep potential (DP) machine-learning force field and the chemical realism of first-principles van der Waals (vdW) corrected hybrid density functional theory (DFT) enabled by a SeA high-throughput framework. Using this approach, we study the structure and dynamics of PE oligomers and polymers in an ethylene solvent under common high-pressure (supercritical) radical polymerization conditions. We found that the local solvation environment of radical-containing PE oligomers converges for chain lengths greater than (n~6), suggesting extensibility of our oligomer-trained MLFF to significantly longer polymers. We then confirmed the extensibility of these models to long PE chains by characterizing the molecular weight scaling of single-chain structure and dynamics, which showed classic good solvent behavior. Our PE MLFF retained a consistent level of fidelity and stability across a wide range of thermodynamic state points and chain lengths, at full atomistic resolution, therefore paving the way towards first-principles-based polymer structure and property prediction.

    💡 亮点：聚乙烯的微观原子结构在非高度晶态体系中难以通过实验精确确定，经验势虽高效，却可能缺乏复杂反应过程所需的化学真实性。本文结合深度势机器学习力场与经范德华修正的杂化密度泛函理论，研究超临界自由基聚合条件下乙烯溶剂中的聚乙烯低聚物和聚合物。结果显示，含自由基聚乙烯低聚物的局部溶剂化环境在链长超过约 6 个单元后趋于收敛，模型可扩展至更长链，并再现了经典良溶剂行为。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：作者结合深度势机器学习力场与含范德华修正的杂化密度泛函，研究超临界自由基聚合条件下乙烯溶剂、聚乙烯低聚物和长链聚合物的原子结构与动力学。训练数据必须覆盖自由基端基、链增长、C–C成键、溶剂化和不同链长构型，力场随后用于超越直接混合泛函MD的时间与尺度限制。结果显示链长超过约6个单元后局部溶剂化环境收敛，并能再现经典良溶剂行为；具体势函数和训练集规模未在摘要中给出。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该工作属于反应性机器学习力场、深度势和高压聚合分子动力学，和普通非反应经验势相比，优势在于能够描述键断裂、成键及自由基局域电子环境。其“短链到长链外推”与材料研究中从小胞元训练势、再预测畴壁或缺陷长程演化相似，但高压化学反应对数据覆盖和外推稳定性要求更高。团队五位研究人员的具体既有方向没有列出，相关接口可放在聚合物、缺陷反应、界面溶剂化、势函数开发和高压模拟五个方向。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可将反应势迁移到NbOI₂表面有机修饰、BaTiO₃聚合物复合界面或CrI₃封装层的化学降解问题。数据集应包含表面吸附、自由基攻击、氧/水参与、层间范德华作用和不同外加应变，参考标签采用自旋极化DFT+U与vdW修正泛函；用不确定度驱动主动学习补充断键和电荷转移构型。验证包括反应势垒、键长分布、界面结合能、扩散系数、MD中的产物比例，并与静态DFT路径和原位光谱特征交叉检查。

    通过张量网络实现连续时间生成模型的可扩展量子模拟#
    Scalable quantum simulation of continuous-time generative models via tensor networks#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Nathan X. Kodama, L. Andrew Wray, Sam Cochran, Chad Rigetti, Shravan Veerapaneni, Michael J. Keiser🔥 AI 96
    相关度
    9.5

    📄 摘要：连续时间流模型和扩散模型已广泛应用于许多领域，从计算机视觉和蛋白质折叠中的大规模部署，到语言、时间序列和量子态建模中的新兴应用。训练完成后，从连续时间模型中推断统计性质的成本很高。波函数流针对这一成本，将学习到的输运过程重新表述为幺正演化，使其最终的玻恩分布逼近目标分布。这样可以制备相干的幅度编码状态（qsample），并由量子算法进行后处理，从而相对于蒙特卡洛采样获得二次加速。我们开展了对这类流的首次数值研究，其中使用张量网络表示随时间变化的势和状态。在空间维度d=8时，与包含N^d个点的稠密网格相比，存储量降低了约10⁷倍；与根据实测的d≤5标度外推得到的基线相比，演化的实际运行时间降低了至少10³倍。我们通过复现稀有事件采样的O(1/√prare)标度验证了整个流程。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21700v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Continuous-time flow and diffusion models are widely used across many application domains, from large-scale deployment in computer vision and protein folding to emerging adoption for modeling language, time series, and quantum states. After training, inferring statistical properties from continuous-time models is costly. Wavefunction flows target this cost by recasting learned transport as unitary evolution, whose final Born distribution approximates the target distribution. This prepares a coherent amplitude encoding (a qsample) that can be post-processed by quantum algorithms offering a quadratic advantage over Monte Carlo sampling. We present the first numerical study of these flows, in which we represent time-dependent potentials and states as tensor networks. At spatial dimension d=8, storage falls by sim 10⁷× relative to the dense grid of N^d points, and evolution wall-clock time falls by gtrsim 10³× against a baseline extrapolated from the measured dłe 5 scaling. We validate our pipeline by reproducing the O(1/sqrtpₘ̊ ᵣₐᵣₑ) scaling of rare-event sampling.

    💡 亮点：连续时间流模型和扩散模型已广泛用于计算机视觉、蛋白质折叠、语言、时间序列及量子态建模，但训练后的统计推断代价较高。本文通过波函数流将学习到的输运重写为幺正演化，使最终的玻恩分布逼近目标分布，并使用张量网络表示随时间变化的势和状态。数值结果显示，在空间维度8时，存储需求相对稠密网格降低约七个数量级，演化时间相对基线外推值降低至少三个数量级，并复现了稀有事件采样的理论标度。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：文章把连续时间流模型或扩散模型学到的输运过程重写成幺正波函数演化，使最终Born分布逼近目标分布，再用张量网络表示随时间变化的势函数和量子态。计算重点是张量网络截断、时间演化和稀有事件采样，而不是直接在稠密网格上存储概率场；在空间维度8的数值测试中，存储需求降低约7个数量级，演化时间相对基线外推至少降低3个数量级，并复现稀有事件采样标度。摘要未给出具体键维、截断误差和训练数据规模。

    🔗 相关工作关联：该方法连接扩散模型、连续正规化流、量子态生成、波函数流和张量网络压缩，差异在于将经典生成输运嵌入幺正演化，从而利用量子态的结构化表示进行统计推断。它与凝聚态中的MPS、TEBD、变分Monte Carlo及神经量子态互补，可处理高维配置空间但会受到纠缠增长和截断误差限制。团队五位研究人员的具体方向未提供，适合衔接量子模拟、铁电相变、磁性采样、生成模型和张量网络五条研究线。

    💡 对你方向的启示：可把BaTiO₃的局域极化、NbOI₂的结构畸变或CrI₃/CrSBr的自旋构型编码为离散或连续格点变量，先用DFT、有效Hamiltonian或MACE/NEP MD产生目标分布，再训练连续时间生成模型学习从简单基分布到相变分布的输运。将波函数和势用MPS或PEPS表示，扫描温度、外场和应变并重点采样畴壁、极化翻转及磁畴核等稀有事件。验证包括边缘分布、两点关联、自由能差、相变温度、稀有事件率和张量截断误差收敛。

    第一性原理人工智能发现分数量子霍尔液体的晶化#
    First-Principles AI finds crystallization of fractional quantum Hall liquids#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ahmed Abouelkomsan, Liang Fu🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    9.5

    📄 摘要：分数量子霍尔（FQH）液体何时会晶化？要回答这一问题，需要一种能够在强 Landau 能级混合区域内平等处理分数化与晶化的框架。在这里，我们引入 MagNet，这是一种为环面几何中磁场下量子系统设计的自注意力神经网络变分波函数。我们表明，MagNet 提供了一个统一且表达能力强的 Ansatz，能够在同一架构内描述 FQH 态和电子晶体。MagNet 仅通过对微观哈密顿量进行能量最小化来训练，并在广泛的 Landau 能级混合范围内自主发现拓扑液体和电子晶体基态。我们的结果凸显了第一性原理人工智能解决强相互作用多体问题、并在没有外部训练数据或预先物理知识的情况下发现竞争相的能力。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2602.03927v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: When does a fractional quantum Hall (FQH) liquid crystallize? Addressing this question requires a framework that treats fractionalization and crystallization on equal footing, especially in strong Landau-level mixing regime. Here, we introduce MagNet, a self-attention neural-network variational wavefunction designed for quantum systems in magnetic fields on the torus geometry. We show that MagNet provides a unifying and expressive ansatz capable of describing both FQH states and electron crystals within the same architecture. Trained solely by energy minimization of the microscopic Hamiltonian, MagNet discovers topological liquid and electron crystal ground states across a broad range of Landau-level mixing. Our results highlight the power of first-principles AI for solving strongly interacting many-body problems and finding competing phases without external training data or physics pre-knowledge.

    💡 亮点：本文研究分数量子霍尔液体何时发生晶化，并提出了用于磁场中环面几何量子系统的自注意力神经网络变分波函数 MagNet。MagNet 在同一架构内统一描述分数量子霍尔态和电子晶体，仅通过微观哈密顿量的能量最小化进行训练。结果表明，该方法能够在广泛的 Landau 能级混合范围内自主发现拓扑液体和电子晶体基态，无需外部训练数据或预先输入物理知识。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：第一性原理标注与结构优化, 有效哈密顿量或机器学习哈密顿量，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《第一性原理人工智能发现分数量子霍尔液体的晶化》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    d电子态密度在Pt–Ni和Pt–Pd纳米颗粒量子尺寸效应中的作用#
    Role of d-electron density of states in the quantum size effect newline of Pt-Ni and Pt-Pd nanoparticles#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 S. Kitagawa, T. Ihara, Y. Kinoshita, K. Ishida, K. Kusada, H. Kitagawa🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：我们利用¹⁹⁵Pt核磁共振测量，研究了双金属Pt₁₋ₓPdₓ和Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ纳米颗粒中的量子尺寸效应（QSE）。Pt₁₋ₓPdₓ纳米颗粒中，核自旋晶格弛豫率除以温度后的异常行为1/T₁T在温度和尺寸上的依赖性表明，Pt原子和Pd原子的电子态相似，并且可以用QSE很好地理解。1/T₁T和Knight位移随温度及组分的变化显示，随着Ni含量增加，态密度系统性升高，而特征能标T*降低，这与Kubo能隙δ_Kubo一致。与Pt₁₋ₓCuₓ纳米颗粒中QSE受到抑制的情况相反，Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ纳米颗粒表现出量子能量离散化的清晰特征。这一差异突出了d电子在QSE显现过程中的关键作用。此外，修正的Korringa参数K(α)分析表明，随着Ni浓度增加，铁磁关联增强，并趋近于铁磁量子临界区域。这些结果为d电子态密度在由金属d电子原子形成的纳米颗粒中QSE显现所发挥的关键作用提供了实验证据。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22743v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We investigated the quantum size effect (QSE) in bimetallic Pt₁₋ₓPdₓ and Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ nanoparticles, using ¹⁹⁵Pt nuclear magnetic resonance measurements. The temperature and size dependencies of the anomaly in the nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate divided by temperature 1/T₁T in the Pt₁₋ₓPdₓ nanoparticles suggest similar electron states between Pt and Pd atoms and are well understood by the QSE. The temperature and composition variations of 1/T₁T and Knight shift reveal a systematic increase in the density of states and reduction of the characteristic energy scale T^* with increasing Ni content, consistent with the Kubo gap δKᵤbₒ. In contrast to Pt₁₋ₓCuₓ nanoparticles where the QSE is suppressed, the Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ nanoparticles exhibit clear signatures of quantum energy discretization. This discrepancy highlights the essential role of d-electrons in the manifestation of the QSE. Furthermore, analysis of the modified Korringa parameter K(α) suggests enhanced ferromagnetic correlations with increasing Ni concentration, approaching a ferromagnetic quantum critical regime. These results provide experimental evidence that d-electron density of states plays a crucial role in the manifestation of the QSE in the nanoparticles formed by the metallic d-electron atoms.

    💡 亮点：本文利用¹⁹⁵Pt核磁共振测量研究了双金属Pt₁₋ₓPdₓ和Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ纳米颗粒中的量子尺寸效应。Pt₁₋ₓPdₓ纳米颗粒中核自旋晶格弛豫率除以温度后的异常行为与量子尺寸效应相符，而Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ体系中随Ni含量增加，态密度系统性增强、特征能标T*降低。与Pt₁₋ₓCuₓ纳米颗粒中量子尺寸效应受到抑制不同，Pt₁₋ₓNiₓ纳米颗粒表现出清晰的量子能级离散化，并显示出增强的铁磁关联，说明d电子态密度对金属纳米颗粒量子尺寸效应的形成至关重要。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    受监测超导链中的测量增强纠缠#
    Measurement-enhanced entanglement in a monitored superconducting chain#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 超导📖 arXiv / cond-mat.supr-con+cond-mat.mtrl-sci+cond-mat.str-el+physics.comp-ph+physics.chem-ph👤 Rui-Jing Guo, Ji-Yao Chen, Zhi-Yuan Wei🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：在受监测量子动力学中，一个普遍观点是局域测量会抑制纠缠增长。我们表明，在一个由BCS哈密顿量控制的一维自旋ful费米子链中，这一直觉可能失效；该链具有配对强度Δ，并以速率γ受到连续的现场、自旋分辨电荷测量。通过自由费米子模拟和准粒子分析，我们发现配对会抑制纠缠增长，而测量会抑制配对。二者之间的竞争产生了测量增强纠缠：当Δ>0时，在有限区间0<γ<γₚₑₐₖ内，稳态纠缠Sₛ会随γ增加。这是因为更强的测量抑制了配对关联，而配对关联原本会抑制纠缠增长。通过非线性σ模型计算和自由费子模拟，我们提供了证据表明，当Δ>0且γ为小的有限值时，稳态纠缠按Sₛ₍L)∼ln²L标度增长。这意味着在该体系中，测量增强纠缠不会在热力学极限下持续存在。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2604.04375v2 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: A common view in monitored quantum dynamics is that local measurements suppress entanglement growth. We show that this intuition can fail in a one-dimensional spinful fermionic chain governed by a BCS Hamiltonian with pairing strength Δ and subject to continuous, on-site, spin-resolved charge measurements at rate γ. Using free-fermion simulations and quasiparticle analysis, we show that pairing suppresses entanglement growth, while measurements suppress pairing. Their competition yields measurement-enhanced entanglement: for Δ>0, the steady-state entanglement Sₛ increases with γ over a finite interval 0<γ<γₚₑₐₖ. This occurs because stronger measurements suppress pairing correlations, which would otherwise suppress entanglement growth. Using a nonlinear sigma-model calculation and free-fermion simulations, we provide evidence that for Δ>0 and small but finite γ, the steady-state entanglement scales as Sₛ₍L)sim łn² L. This implies that, in this setting, measurement-enhanced entanglement does not persist in the thermodynamic limit.

    💡 亮点：通常认为，受监测量子动力学中的局域测量会抑制纠缠增长。本文表明，在具有配对强度Δ的BCS哈密顿量一维自旋ful费米子链中，连续的现场自旋分辨电荷测量可能产生相反效果：配对抑制纠缠增长，而测量抑制配对。二者竞争导致测量增强纠缠，在有限测量速率区间内稳态纠缠随测量速率γ增加；但在小而有限的γ下，稳态纠缠仅按ln²L增长，意味着该增强效应在热力学极限下不会持续。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    文献研究：Universal equilibrium magic in quantum many-body systems#
    Universal equilibrium magic in quantum many-body systems#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 量子信息·计算📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Soumyadeep Sarma, Tobias Haug, John Preskill, Wai-Keong Mok🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22939v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Thermalization conventionally describes local properties of isolated many-body systems at equilibrium. Magic, or nonstabilizerness n̆icodex2013 the resource enabling universal quantum computation n̆icodex2013 is by contrast encoded in the global structure of the many-body wavefunction. We show that, despite its global nature, the magic of equilibrium pure states of chaotic many-body systems, including late-time evolved states and energy eigenstates, is universal: it is captured by the thermal Scrooge ensemble, the minimally informative ensemble of pure states consistent with the Gibbs state at the same effective temperature. Therefore, for systems with no conserved quantities other than the total energy, equilibrium magic is a function of temperature alone, independent of the initial state and other microscopic features of the equilibrium state. This yields concrete universal predictions for the stabilizer Rényi entropies (SREs). At infinite temperature, the SRE is set by Haar-like fluctuations of the Pauli spectrum, while at finite temperature energy conservation induces a volume-law thermodynamic correction controlled by the thermal Pauli spectrum. We support these predictions with analytical arguments and extensive numerical simulations. We further show that chaotic many-body systems at high temperatures possess long-range magic and entanglement that cannot be removed by finite-depth local quantum circuits. Our results establish magic as a thermodynamic property of chaotic many-body systems and suggest that Scrooge ensembles may provide a unified framework for quantum many-body resources.

    文献研究：Analytical Treatment of Noise-Suppressed Klein Tunneling in Graphene with Possible Implications for Quantum-Dot Qubits#
    Analytical Treatment of Noise-Suppressed Klein Tunneling in Graphene with Possible Implications for Quantum-Dot Qubits#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 量子信息·计算📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Kamal Azaidaoui, Ahmed Jellal, Hocine Bahlouli, A. Al Luhaibi, Michael Vogl
    相关度
    8
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2604.23279v4 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We study quantum tunneling through a potential barrier whose height fluctuates in time and is modeled by Gaussian white noise. We map the stochastic dynamics onto an equivalent time-independent Lindblad equation for the density matrix, allowing fully analytical solutions. For Schrödinger particles, noise introduces dissipation that suppresses Fabry-Pérot oscillations and yields an exponentially decaying transmission. Applying the same formalism to graphene, we demonstrate that noise induces a complex longitudinal wavevector within the barrier, leading to a strong suppression of transmission and Klein tunneling, even at normal incidence. Our approach promises improved control over Klein tunneling. These results demonstrate that noisy barriers can act as tunable dissipative elements, offering a pathway to enhanced control of electron transport in graphene-based devices. We also briefly discuss how our results could guide the design of graphene quantum dots for potential use in spin qubit devices.

    横向电场下无限一维晶格的布洛赫态#
    Bloch states of an infinite one-dimensional lattice under transverse electric field#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 拓扑·电子结构📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Anand Aruna Kumar
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：我们考虑一条有效的一维周期性原子链，并令其受到垂直于链轴的弱静态电场作用。内禀晶格在倒空间中由谐波加权的周期性赝势表示，而横向电场产生弱的弹性响应和极化响应，其最小能量分布用周期重复的悬链线形貌近似。布洛赫约化将这两种贡献置于同一个倒空间哈密顿量中。内禀晶格产生(|r-s|⁻¹⁾层级，而悬链线贡献则是一个对称托普利兹修正，其系数与((1+π²(r-2)²⁾⁻¹⁾成正比，因此渐近上表现为(|r-s|⁻²⁾关系。悬链线贡献的全部非零倒空间权重绝对收敛，并求和为|V̄c|/e。在晶胞层面，最低阶内禀谐波与悬链线项共同给出一个同时包含余弦和双曲余弦调制的马修方程。随后进行第一布里渊区约化，得到场依赖的能带能量及相应的布拉格带隙层级。该模型揭示了一种简单机制，即横向电场能够重组低维周期性体系的布洛赫谱。关键词：布洛赫理论；横向电场；悬链线势；马修方程；布里渊区；能带结构。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22199v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: We consider an effectively one-dimensional periodic atomic chain subjected to a weak static electric field transverse to the chain axis. The intrinsic lattice is represented in reciprocal space by a harmonically weighted periodic pseudopotential, while the transverse field produces a weak elastic and polarizable response whose minimum-energy distribution is approximated by a periodically repeated catenary profile. Bloch reduction places both contributions in a common reciprocal-space Hamiltonian. The intrinsic lattice produces a (|r-s|^ -1 ) hierarchy, whereas the catenary contribution is a symmetric Toeplitz correction with coefficients proportional to ((1+π²⁽r-2) ^ -1 ) and therefore asymptotically to (|r-s|^ -2 ). The complete nonzero reciprocal weight of the catenary contribution is absolutely convergent and sums to (|V_ m̊ c |/e ). At the unit-cell level, the lowest intrinsic harmonic together with the catenary term gives a Mathieu equation containing both cosine and hyperbolic-cosine modulations. A first-Brillouin-zone reduction then yields the field-dependent band energies and the corresponding hierarchy of Bragg gaps. The model isolates a simple mechanism by which a transverse electric field can reorganize the Bloch spectrum of a low-dimensional periodic system. Keywords: Bloch theory; transverse electric field; catenary potential; Mathieu equation; Brillouin zone; band structure.

    💡 亮点：本文考虑受弱静态横向电场作用的有效一维周期性原子链。研究将内禀晶格和横向电场引起的弹性、极化响应统一表示在倒空间哈密顿量中，其中后者由周期重复的悬链线形貌近似。模型显示，横向场引入的修正具有对称托普利兹结构，并可在晶胞尺度导出同时含余弦和双曲余弦调制的马修方程，进而得到场依赖能带能量及布拉格带隙层级。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    真实相互作用下阿贝尔分数量子霍尔相中的非阿贝尔编织#
    Non-Abelian braiding in Abelian Fractional Quantum Hall Phases from realistic interactions#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat.supr-con+cond-mat.mtrl-sci+cond-mat.str-el+physics.comp-ph+physics.chem-ph👤 Ha Quang Trung, Bo Yang
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：我们提出了一种方法，在ν=1/3的Laughlin分数量子霍尔相中，利用最低Landau能级内真实的二体相互作用实现分数化准空穴的非阿贝尔编织。数值结果表明，ν=1/3附近的低能隙激发几乎完全包含在三体Moore–Read模型哈密顿量的零空间内。因此，它们是原则上具有物理可及性的非阿贝尔准空穴量子流体。特别地，Laughlin基态可以描述为由“ψ型”准空穴组成的流体，这些准空穴通过将磁通与一个Majorana费米子（MF）结合而形成；而Laughlin准空穴则由“1型”准空穴描述，即未附着MF的磁通。在Laughlin相内，当准空穴之间的强吸引被适当设计的一体电子静电俘获势克服时，Laughlin准空穴可以在局域上分数化为非阿贝尔准空穴。采用适当有限尺寸标度的广泛数值计算支持了这一物理图像；我们的研究指出，无需对真实电子—电子相互作用进行精细调节，便有可能在实验中于阿贝尔拓扑相内实现非阿贝尔编织。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2606.07517v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We propose a method of realizing non-Abelian braiding of fractionalized quasiholes in the Laughlin fractional quantum Hall phase at ν=1/3 with realistic two-body interactions within the lowest Landau level. It is numerically shown that low-lying gapped excitations near ν=1/3 are contained almost entirely within the null space of the three-body Moore-Read model Hamiltonian. They are thus quantum fluids of non-Abelian quasiholes that are in principle physically accessible. In particular, Laughlin ground state can be described as a fluid of ``ψ-type" quasiholes formed by binding a magnetic flux with a Majorana fermion (MF), and the Laughlin quasiholes are described by the ``1-type'' quasiholes, which are magnetic fluxes without a MF attached. Within the Laughlin phase, Laughlin quasiholes can be locally fractionalized into non-Abelian quasiholes, when the strong attraction between them is overcome by properly designed one-body electronstatic trapping potentials. Extensive numerics with proper finite-size scaling corroborate this physical picture, and our study points to the possibility of realizing non-Abelian braiding within an Abelian topological phase in experiment without the need for fine-tuning realistic electron-electron interaction.

    💡 亮点：本文提出了一种在ν=1/3的Laughlin分数量子霍尔相中，利用最低Landau能级内真实的二体相互作用实现分数化准空穴非阿贝尔编织的方法。数值结果显示，ν=1/3附近的低能隙激发几乎完全位于三体Moore–Read模型哈密顿量的零空间中，因此可被视为物理上原则可及的非阿贝尔准空穴量子流体。研究表明，通过适当设计的一体电子静电俘获势，可以在不需要微调真实电子相互作用的情况下，在阿贝尔拓扑相内实现非阿贝尔编织。

    从波函数快照研究多体遍历性破缺#
    Many-body ergodicity breaking from wavefunction snapshots#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Riccardo Andreoni, Devendra Singh Bhakuni, Marcello Dalmonte, Lev Vidmar, Miroslav Hopjan🔥 AI 97
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：现代量子实验可以通过投影测量探测多体波函数，从而获得单个多体构型的快照。一个基本问题是：测量分布的内在结构是否能够揭示超越预先定义观测量的量子动力学。本文在多体遍历性破缺转变处，使用非平衡波函数快照的两个互补特征来回答这一问题：二元内禀维数（BID），以及由汉明距离构建的波函数网络的拓扑结构。我们表明，临界点的特征是广延但低于最大值的BID，以及具有高度连接枢纽的无标度网络连通性。这些特征能够区分遍历、临界和非遍历区域，并为利用投影测量快照实验探测遍历性破缺提供可行手段。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21524v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Modern quantum experiments can probe many-body wavefunctions through projective measurements, providing snapshots of individual many-body configurations. A fundamental question is whether the intrinsic structure of their measurement distributions can reveal quantum dynamics beyond predefined observables. Here, we address this question at the many-body ergodicity-breaking transition using two complementary characteristics of nonequilibrium wavefunction snapshots: their binary intrinsic dimension (BID) and the topology of wavefunction networks constructed from Hamming distances. We show that the critical point is characterized by an extensive but submaximal BID, and scale-free network connectivity with highly connected hubs. These signatures distinguish the ergodic, critical, and nonergodic regimes, and provide experimentally accessible probes of ergodicity breaking from projective measurement snapshots.

    💡 亮点：现代量子实验能够通过投影测量获得单个多体构型的波函数快照。本文在多体遍历性破缺转变处，利用二元内禀维数和基于汉明距离构建的波函数网络，研究测量分布能否揭示超越预设观测量的量子动力学。结果表明，临界点具有广延但非最大化的内禀维数，以及带有高度连接枢纽的无标度网络结构，这些特征可区分遍历、临界和非遍历区域。

    稀薄非相对论中子气体中的等离激元-极化激元#
    Plasmon-polaritons in a rarefied nonrelativistic neutron gas#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 P. O. Kazinski, A. M. Trushchuk🔥 AI 91
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：arXiv:2608.22177v1；公告类型：新提交。本文研究在中子-中子散射可以忽略的情况下，稀薄、非极化、非相对论中子气体的电磁性质。研究考虑了中子气体的高斯（麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼）和费米-狄拉克一粒子密度矩阵，并假定这些密度矩阵变化的典型空间尺度远大于电磁场变化的典型空间尺度。研究获得了静态有效麦克斯韦方程的格林函数。研究确定了非平衡中子气体具有铁磁不稳定性时的参数。研究描述了类似弗里德尔振荡的现象。研究揭示了中子气体中等离激元-极化激元的性质。结果表明，纵向和横向等离激元-极化激元的色散关系在给定动量下具有无穷多个分支。研究确定了等离激元-极化激元可以被视为准粒子的参数区域。研究描述了单个电子的高斯波包上的等离激元-极化激元。事实证明，它们的色散关系在给定动量下同样具有无穷多个分支。研究表明，即使在单个电子上也存在稳定的纵向等离激元-极化激元。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22177v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The electromagnetic properties of a dilute unpolarized nonrelativistic neutron gas are described in the case when neutron-by-neutron scattering is negligible. The Gaussian (Maxwell-Boltzmann) and Fermi-Dirac one-particle density matrices for the neutron gas are considered, the typical space scale of variations of these density matrices being assumed to be much larger than the the typical space scale of variations of the electromagnetic field. The Green functions for the static effective Maxwell equations are obtained. The parameters of a nonequilibrium neutron gas are found where this gas possesses a ferromagnetic instability. The analog of Friedel oscillations is described. The properties of plasmon-polaritons in the neutron gas are revealed. It turns out that the dispersion law of longitudinal and transverse plasmon-polaritons has an infinite number of branches at a given momentum. The region of parameters where the plasmon-polaritons can be regarded as quasiparticles is found. The plasmon-polaritons on a Gaussian wave packet of a single electron are described. Their dispersion law proves to have an infinite number of branches at a given momentum. It is shown that there exist stable longitudinal plasmon-polaritons even on a single electron.

    💡 亮点：本文研究中子间散射可忽略时，稀薄、非极化、非相对论中子气体的电磁性质，并分别考虑高斯分布和费米-狄拉克分布的一粒子密度矩阵。研究获得了静态有效麦克斯韦方程的格林函数，确定了非平衡中子气体发生铁磁不稳定性的参数范围，并描述了类似弗里德尔振荡的现象。结果表明，纵向和横向等离激元-极化激元在给定动量下具有无穷多个色散分支，且即使在单个电子上也存在稳定的纵向等离激元-极化激元。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    Ewald 求和的模型密度方法#
    Model density approach to Ewald summations#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 其他📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Chiara Ribaldone, Jacques Kontak Desmarais
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：静电势的计算对于凝聚相体系的研究至关重要。我们讨论了通过 Ewald 型技术计算相关晶格求和的方法。我们引入一种模型电荷密度，用于抵消晶体电荷分布截至所需阶数的多极矩，从而加速 Ewald 求和的收敛。该方法适用于采用任意晶胞的体相体系，既适用于经典情形，也适用于量子情形，并且允许使用任意基函数来表示电荷密度。我们以块体半导体砷化镓的基本带隙计算作为原型示例，展示了该方法的有效性；数值结果证实，由于需要计算的双电子积分数量减少，收敛速度得到了显著提升。该方法还澄清了 CRYSTAL 程序中一项存在数十年之久的实现。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2601.21776v4 Announce Type: replace Abstract: The evaluation of the electrostatic potential is fundamental to the study of condensed phase systems. We discuss the calculation of the relevant lattice summations by Ewald-type techniques. A model charge density is introduced, that cancels multipole moments of the crystalline charge distribution up to a desired order, for accelerating convergence of the Ewald sums. The method is applicable to calculations of bulk systems, employing arbitrary unit cells in a classical or quantum context, and with arbitrary basis functions to represent the charge density. The efficacy of the method is demonstrated on the calculation of the fundamental gap of the gallium arsenide bulk semiconductor, as a prototype example, where significantly accelerated convergence is numerically confirmed, due to a reduction of the number of two-electron integrals that need to be computed. The approach clarifies a decades-old implementation in the CRYSTAL code.

    💡 亮点：静电势的计算是研究凝聚相体系的基础。本文讨论了利用 Ewald 型技术计算相关晶格求和，并引入一种模型电荷密度，用于抵消晶体电荷分布截至指定阶数的多极矩，从而加速 Ewald 求和的收敛。该方法适用于具有任意晶胞、处于经典或量子情形下的体相体系，也适用于表示电荷密度的任意基函数；在块体砷化镓带隙计算中的数值结果显示，其可通过减少所需计算的双电子积分数量显著加快收敛。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，摘要目前只能确认：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法。需要回到原文核对数据来源、标签定义、模型架构、物理约束和验证集，不能把题名直接等同于可迁移算法。若要接入团队的 ML 势或 Hamiltonian 流程，应先确认它是否同时学习能量、力、应力、自旋、极化或电子结构，以及是否报告跨材料/跨温度外推。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    Optical-Fiber Activation of Observer-Split Source Terms in a Rotating Aluminum Disc#

A covariantly conserved current, rewritten in a rotating 3+1 split, produces the observer-adapted source term Iₘ̊ ₛₚₗᵢₜ=N⁻¹Dᵢ₍ₕ̊oβⁱ⁾, which in the weak-field rigid-rotation limit becomes I_G=Ømegapartial_φh̊o. The work is a falsifiable protocol for testing anomalous gravitational-response signatures; it reports no anomalous-weight measurement and makes no claim of evidence for a gravitational anomaly or for extended Aharonov-Bohm electrodynamics.

    文献研究：Quantum observables as Fréchet sensitivity kernels#
    Quantum observables as Fréchet sensitivity kernels#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P1 · 其他📖 arXiv / physics👤 Rafael Abreu🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    7
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23191v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: We present a variational--adjoint interpretation of quantum mechanics in which the interaction between forward and adjoint wavefunctions defines a general sensitivity kernel of the system. This Fréchet interaction density quantifies how perturbations in the wavefunction (or system parameters) influence a chosen observable. The familiar Born probability density appears as a special case when the adjoint wavefunction is chosen as the complex conjugate of the forward wavefunction, for which the interaction density becomes real and non-negative. Within this framework, probability is a particular positive-definite form of sensitivity described by the Fréchet interaction density. In general, the variational--adjoint interpretation also produces Fréchet sensitivity kernels associated with other quantum observables, including momentum, energy, and spin. This suggests that the Born probability density belongs to a broader class of Fréchet sensitivity kernels associated with quantum observables. The proposed interpretation also provides a connection with time-symmetric interpretations of quantum mechanics and possible future applications in quantum control and quantum metrology.

    研究铁电薄膜中不同畴演化的新型解耦方法#
    A Novel Decoupling Method for Investigating Distinct Domain Evolution in Ferroelectric Film#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Keying Sun, Jiajun Qiu, Hao Li, Zongwei Shang, Lang Zeng, Ming Li, Lining Zhang, Runsheng Wang🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：传统的极化表征技术只能提供铁电薄膜的平均响应，从而限制了对依赖畴的可靠性机制的研究。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的畴解耦方法，用于分别分析具有不同开关行为的铁电畴。首先引入一个三畴模型，其中包括向上不可切换畴、向下不可切换畴以及可切换畴（Pd），以描述电循环过程中的非均匀畴群。通过结合互补的开关电流测量，可以选择性地提取和重构不同畴群的响应。所提出的方法能够在电循环过程中定量追踪各个畴群的数量。作为示例，我们进一步利用该方法分析电循环之后畴群随时间的演化。该方法为研究铁电器件中的印记、疲劳以及其他与可靠性相关的现象提供了新的途径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22755v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Conventional polarization characterization techniques provide only the averaged response of ferroelectric films, limiting the investigation of domain-dependent reliability mechanisms in HfO2-based ferroelectrics. In this work, a new domain decoupling method is proposed to separately analyze ferroelectric domains with distinct switching behaviors. A three-domain model consisting of upward non-switchable domains, downward non-switchable domains, and switchable domains (Pd) is first introduced to describe heterogeneous domain populations during electrical cycling. By combining complementary switching-current measurements, the responses of different domain populations can be selectively extracted and reconstructed. The proposed method enables quantitative tracking of individual domain populations during cycling. As a demonstration, the method is further applied to analyze the time-dependent evolution of domain populations after electrical cycling. This approach provides a new route for investigating imprint, fatigue, and other reliability-related phenomena in ferroelectric devices.

    💡 亮点：传统极化表征技术只能提供铁电薄膜的平均响应，因此难以研究与畴相关的可靠性机制。本文提出一种畴解耦方法，通过包含向上不可切换畴、向下不可切换畴和可切换畴（Pd）的三畴模型，结合互补的开关电流测量，分别提取并重构不同畴群的响应。该方法可定量追踪电循环过程中各类畴的演化，并用于分析电循环后的时间依赖性畴演变，为研究印记、疲劳等铁电器件可靠性现象提供了新途径。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    TiNiSi结构家族中的金属d波交错磁性图谱#
    Mapping metallic d-wave altermagnetism across the TiNiSi structural family#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Zhen Zhang, Subhadip Pradhan, Kirill D. Belashchenko, Vladimir Antropov🔥 AI 86
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：利用交错磁体作为可切换的垂直偏振自旋流源的前景，推动了对候选材料的持续搜寻；然而，具有显著自旋分裂器响应的金属d波体系仍然十分稀缺。在本文中，我们识别出一种能够在TiNiSi结构家族中支持金属d波交错磁性的经验磁性构型：铁磁有序的之字形链之间具有反铁磁链间耦合，并处于相对低对称性的晶体环境中。TiNiSi结构类型兼具这一磁性构型、广泛的化学可调性以及相互竞争的磁基态。通过结合对280种三元体系的热力学稳定性和磁基态进行第一性原理筛选，我们识别出16种金属d波交错磁体。这一集合重新获得了4种实验上已知的成员，即WFeB、NbMnP、TaMnP和NbMnAs，并给出了12种新的预测材料，其中ScMnP、TaMnAs、ScMnAs、MoMnAs、MoMnSi和WMnSi最具潜力。非共线计算表明，在这些体系中，共线交错磁构型均为基态。六种最有前景的新候选物均具有非零自旋分裂角和有限的反常霍尔电导率。值得注意的是，ScMnP和TaMnAs尽管具有适中的交错磁能带分裂，却表现出很强的自旋分裂响应。这些结果确立了TiNiSi型金属d波交错磁体作为高效电荷到自旋转换的化学多样化平台，并提供了一条由经验磁性构型指导进一步寻找候选材料的路径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23409v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The prospect of using altermagnets as switchable sources of perpendicularly polarized spin currents has intensified the search for candidate materials, yet metallic d-wave systems with sizable spin-splitter responses remain scarce. Here, we identify an empirical magnetic motif that supports metallic d-wave altermagnetism in the TiNiSi structural family: ferromagnetically ordered zigzag chains with antiferromagnetic interchain coupling in a relatively low-symmetry crystal environment. The TiNiSi structure type combines this motif with broad chemical flexibility and competing magnetic ground states. By combining first-principles screening of thermodynamic stability and magnetic ground states across 280 ternary systems, we identify 16 metallic d-wave altermagnets. This set recovers four experimentally known members---WFeB, NbMnP, TaMnP, and NbMnAs---and yields 12 new predictions, of which ScMnP, TaMnAs, ScMnAs, MoMnAs, MoMnSi, and WMnSi are the most promising. Noncollinear calculations indicate that the collinear altermagnetic configuration is the ground state across them. Each of the six leading new candidates has a nonzero spin-splitter angle and a finite anomalous Hall conductivity. Notably, ScMnP and TaMnAs exhibit strong spin-splitter responses despite modest altermagnetic band splitting. These results establish TiNiSi-type metallic d-wave altermagnets as a chemically versatile platform for efficient charge-to-spin conversion and provide an empirical magnetic-motif-guided route to identifying further candidates.

    💡 亮点：本文识别出一种能够在TiNiSi结构家族中支持金属d波交错磁性的经验磁性构型：铁磁排列的之字形链之间以反铁磁方式耦合，并处于相对低对称性的晶体环境中。通过对280种三元体系进行热力学稳定性和磁基态的第一性原理筛选，研究发现16种金属d波交错磁体，其中包括4种已知材料和12种新预测材料。六种最有前景的新候选物均具有非零自旋分裂角和有限反常霍尔电导率，其中ScMnP和TaMnAs虽具有适中的交错磁能带分裂，却表现出强自旋分裂响应。

    共线反铁磁多铁体中的奇宇称手性磁振子#
    Odd-Parity Chiral Magnons in Collinear Antiferromagnetic Multiferroics#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat.supr-con+cond-mat.mtrl-sci+cond-mat.str-el+physics.comp-ph+physics.chem-ph👤 Quanchao Du, Zhenlong Zhang, Yuanjun Jin, Rui Li, Haibo Xie, Zhe Wang, Zhijun Jiang, Lei Zhang, Hongjian Zhao, Jinyang Ni
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：奇宇称磁性代表了非常规磁体研究中一个引人关注的前沿方向；然而，其实现传统上依赖于伴随自旋守恒破缺的非共线磁序，这不可避免地会导致自旋弛豫以及以自旋编码的信息的耗散输运。在此，我们揭示了一条在共线反铁磁多铁体中实现保持自旋守恒的奇宇称磁振子劈裂的新途径，该途径由反手性类霍尔丹通量（antichiral Haldane-like flux，AHF）实现。对称性分析表明，由子晶格内Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）驱动的这种AHF能够产生依赖对称性的奇宇称磁振子劈裂，其形式从p波和f波到无节点形式不等。更进一步，铁电模式与DMI之间的耦合为利用电场操控手性带劈裂和磁振子输运提供了调节手段。结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，我们确定了二维和块体反铁磁多铁体中的一系列有前景候选材料。本工作为在共线反铁磁体中实现奇宇称手性磁振子以及理解多铁体中的磁电耦合机制提供了新的认识。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2605.22294v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Odd-parity magnetism represents an intriguing frontier in unconventional magnets; however, its realization has traditionally relied on noncollinear magnetic orders accompanied by broken spin conservation, which inevitably causes spin relaxation and dissipative transport of spin-encoded information. Here, we uncover a distinct route toward spin-conserving odd-parity magnon splitting enabled by antichiral Haldane-like flux (AHF) in collinear antiferromagnetic multiferroics. Symmetry analysis reveals that such AHF driven by intra-sublattice Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions (DMI) gives rise to symmetry-dependent odd-parity magnon splittings, ranging from p-wave and f-wave to nodeless forms. Moreover, the coupling between ferroelectric modes and DMI provides an electric-field knob for manipulating chiral band splitting and magnon transport. Combining density functional theory (DFT) calculations, we identify a series of promising candidate materials in both 2D and bulk antiferromagnetic multiferroics. Our work provides new insights into the realization of odd-parity chiral magnons in collinear antiferromagnets and magnetoelectric coupling mechanisms in multiferroics.

    💡 亮点：本文揭示了共线反铁磁多铁体中由反手性类霍尔丹通量驱动的、保持自旋守恒的奇宇称磁振子劈裂机制。对称性分析表明，子晶格内的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用可产生从p波、f波到无节点形式的多种磁振子劈裂。铁电模式与DMI之间的耦合还提供了利用电场调控手性带劈裂和磁振子输运的途径，并通过DFT计算筛选出二维和块体候选材料。

    伪Jahn–Teller效应驱动GeTe中的位移型铁电相变#
    Pseudo Jahn-Teller effect driven displacive ferroelectric transition in GeTe#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 Phys. Rev. Materials👤 Changrui Wang, Kaiqi Li, Jian Zhou, and Zhimei Sun🔥 AI 100
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：在本研究中，作者将密度泛函理论与由神经进化势驱动的大规模分子动力学模拟相结合，研究GeTe中的实时原子动力学和局域结构涨落。他们的研究结果表明，GeTe在三临界点附近发生位移型铁电相变。关键的是，计算得到的纵向电流关联函数缺少准弹性峰，从而有效排除了热激活离散跳跃。进一步的时间分辨轨道分析揭示了由伪Jahn–Teller效应驱动的飞秒尺度“跷跷板式”电荷转移。这些结果支持一种“宏观有序、微观无序”的位移型范式。

    📖 英文原文

    Author(s): Changrui Wang, Kaiqi Li, Jian Zhou, and Zhimei Sun In this study, the authors investigate the real-time atomic dynamics and local structural fluctuations in GeTe by coupling density functional theory with large-scale molecular dynamics simulations driven by neuroevolution potentials. Their findings demonstrate a displacive ferroelectric phase transition near the tricritical point. Crucially, the computed longitudinal current correlation function lacks a quasielastic peak, effectively ruling out thermally activated discrete jumps. Furthermore, time-resolved orbital analyses reveal a femtosecond ‘seesaw’ charge transfer driven by the pseudo-Jahn-Teller effect. These results support a ‘macro-ordered yet micro-disordered’ displacive paradigm. [Phys. Rev. Materials 10, 084408] Published Tue Aug 25, 2026

    💡 亮点：本文将密度泛函理论与神经进化势驱动的大规模分子动力学模拟相结合，研究GeTe中的实时原子动力学和局域结构涨落。结果表明，GeTe在三临界点附近发生位移型铁电相变；纵向电流关联函数中缺少准弹性峰，排除了热激活离散跳跃的可能性。时间分辨轨道分析进一步揭示了由伪Jahn–Teller效应驱动的飞秒尺度“跷跷板式”电荷转移，支持“宏观有序、微观无序”的位移型相变图景。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题。从摘要能确认的线索包括：分子动力学与有限温度轨迹, 密度泛函理论和电子结构计算，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到HfO₂、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂、二维范德华铁电、钙钛矿氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 声子、有限温度和输运性质, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在HfO₂、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂、二维范德华铁电、钙钛矿氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”补充训练/验证集，再比较《伪Jahn–Teller效应驱动GeTe中的位移型铁电相变》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    铁电材料中编织畴形成的机制相场建模#
    Mechanistic Phase-Field Modelling of Woven-Domain Formation in Ferroelectric Material#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 P G Kubendran Amos🔥 AI 99
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：近期的一项实验研究报道，在足够缓慢地冷却通过铁电相变时，块体KTN:Li中会自发形成三维编织状铁电畴结构。本文建立了一个无量纲相场框架，用于考察这种状态可能通过何种物理上合理的路径产生。首先，一个大面积二维模型通过结合一级铁电热力学、电静力相互作用、弹性相互作用、组分调制和有效电荷屏蔽，解析了依赖冷却速率形成的受挫交叉前驱结构。慢冷却会产生持久存在的电荷关联交叉，而相应的快速冷却路径不会产生这种结构。随后，三维扩展模型引入立方梯度各向异性、挠曲电耦合和应变梯度正则化，以考察平面前驱结构是否能够发展为真正的编织拓扑。在所探索的无量纲参数范围内，慢冷却会直接形成可分辨的几何分离，以及相交畴壁族之间深度排序的交换，而相应的快速冷却路径不会出现这些现象。这一区别在相互独立的实现中均得到重现，并且对时间步长加密和计算区域尺寸变化保持稳健。模拟表明，慢冷却为拓扑选择提供了一个动力学窗口，同时全局电荷弛豫可以与幸存交叉处的强局部电荷集中共存。该模型没有使用材料特定的KTN:Li系数进行标定，也不能再现实验中观察到的编织结构在低温下消失的现象。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22776v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: A recent experimental study reports the spontaneous formation of a three-dimensional woven ferroelectric domain fabric in bulk KTN:Li during sufficiently slow cooling through the ferroelectric transition. The present work develops a nondimensional phase-field framework to examine a physically plausible route by which such a state can emerge. A large-area two-dimensional model first resolves the cooling-rate-dependent formation of a frustrated crossing precursor by combining first-order ferroelectric thermodynamics, electrostatic and elastic interactions, compositional modulation, and effective charge screening. Slow cooling produces a persistent population of charge-associated crossings, whereas the corresponding fast-cooling pathway does not. A three-dimensional extension then incorporates cubic gradient anisotropy, flexoelectric coupling, and strain-gradient regularization to examine whether the planar precursor can develop a genuine woven topology. Within the explored nondimensional parameter regime, slow cooling produces a directly resolved geometrical separation and exchange of depth ordering between the intersecting wall families, whereas the corresponding fast-cooling pathway does not. This distinction is reproduced across independent realisations and remains robust to timestep refinement and changes in computational domain size. The simulations suggest that slow cooling provides a kinetic window for topological selection and that global charge relaxation can coexist with strong local charge concentration at surviving crossings. The model is not calibrated to material-specific KTN:Li coefficients and does not reproduce the experimentally observed low-temperature disappearance of the woven state.

    💡 亮点：近期实验研究发现，体KТN:Li在足够缓慢地冷却通过铁电相变时，会自发形成三维编织状铁电畴结构。本文建立无量纲相场框架，研究这一结构可能的形成路径：二维模型显示，慢冷却可在热力学、电静力、弹性、组分调制和电荷屏蔽共同作用下形成持久的受挫交叉前驱结构；三维模型进一步表明，梯度各向异性、挠曲电耦合和应变梯度正则化可促成交叉畴壁族之间的深度排序交换。模拟提示，慢冷却为拓扑选择提供动力学窗口，但该模型未针对具体材料参数标定，也未再现实验中低温编织结构消失的现象。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开。从摘要能确认的线索包括：热力学自由能、相稳定与有限温度建模，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到HfO₂ 基铁电、二维滑移铁电、钙钛矿和磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    BaFe₂X₃（X = S 和 Se）中磁有序、结构稳定性与轨道有序的相互作用#
    Interplay of Magnetic Order, Structural Stability, and Orbital Ordering in BaFe2X3 (X = S and Se)#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Kunihiko Yamauchi, Takuya Aoyama, Kenya Ohgushi🔥 AI 96
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：BaFe₂X₃（X = S 和 Se）是具有梯状结构的准一维莫特绝缘体，分别表现出条纹型和区块型反铁磁有序。Fe 原子的梯状排列和强电子关联产生了丰富的磁性和轨道现象，包括压力诱导的超导性和轨道选择性电子态。两种化合物在 Néel 温度以上也都表现出电阻率异常，这表明轨道自由度在其电子性质中发挥了重要作用。为阐明磁有序、结构稳定性、轨道有序和输运性质之间的相互作用，我们对 BaFe₂S₃ 和 BaFe₂Se₃ 进行了电阻率测量和第一性原理计算，并系统比较了候选磁结构和晶体结构。我们发现，磁构型会强烈影响稳定晶体结构、轨道有序和输运各向异性，从而为理解两种化合物不同的电子性质提供了微观解释。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23081v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: BaFe2X3 (X= S and Se) are quasi-one-dimensional Mott insulators with a ladder structure that exhibit Stripe- and Block-type antiferromagnetic order, respectively. The ladder arrangement of Fe atoms and strong electron correlations give rise to rich magnetic and orbital phenomena, including pressure-induced superconductivity and orbital-selective electronic states. Both compounds also show resistivity anomalies above the Neel temperature, suggesting that orbital degrees of freedom play an important role in their electronic properties. To clarify the interplay among magnetic order, structural stability, orbital ordering, and transport properties, we performed resistivity measurements and first-principles calculations for BaFe2S3 and BaFe2Se3, systematically comparing candidate magnetic and crystal structures. We find that the magnetic configuration strongly influences the stable crystal structure, orbital ordering, and transport anisotropy, providing a microscopic understanding of the contrasting electronic properties of the two compounds.

    💡 亮点：BaFe₂S₃ 和 BaFe₂Se₃ 是具有梯状结构的准一维莫特绝缘体，分别表现出条纹型和区块型反铁磁有序。本文结合电阻率测量与第一性原理计算，系统比较不同候选磁结构和晶体结构。结果表明，磁构型会强烈影响稳定晶体结构、轨道有序和输运各向异性，从而解释了两种化合物不同的电子性质。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    相互作用介导的交错磁性与拓扑共存#
    Interaction-mediated Co-existence of Altermagnetism and Topology#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Jasmin Bedow, Nitin Kaushal, Marcel Franz🔥 AI 84
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：我们研究了Lieb晶格上电子填充数为2和4的扩展Hubbard模型，并证明相互作用能够同时诱导交错磁有序和拓扑能隙。利用无约束Hartree-Fock理论，我们计算了交错磁序参量和拓扑序参量，并表明在相图的较大区域内二者共存，同时伴随量子自旋霍尔效应；我们进一步利用精确对角化对此进行了支持。此外，我们证明，破缺反演对称性的项也能够介导二阶拓扑相，并产生相应的角态。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23442v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We study an extended Hubbard model on Lieb lattice at electron filling 2 and 4, and demonstrate that the interactions lead to the simultaneous emergence of altermagnetic order and a topological gap. Using unrestricted Hartree-Fock theory, we evaluate both the altermagnetic and topological order parameters and show that they co-exist with an associated quantum spin Hall effect in a large region of the phase diagram, which we further support using exact diagonalization. Moreover, we demonstrate that inversion-symmetry breaking terms can also mediate second-order topological phases with associated corner modes.

    💡 亮点：本文研究了Lieb晶格上电子填充数为2和4的扩展Hubbard模型，证明电子相互作用可以同时诱导交错磁有序和拓扑能隙。无约束Hartree-Fock计算显示，在相图的较大区域内，交错磁序与拓扑序共存，并伴随量子自旋霍尔效应，精确对角化结果进一步支持了这一结论。研究还表明，破缺反演对称性的项能够介导二阶拓扑相，并产生相应的角态。

    范德瓦耳斯亚铁电薄膜中声子与铁子（ferron）的挠曲应变工程#
    Flexo-Strain Engineering of Phonons and Ferrons in Thin Films of Van der Waals Ferrielectrics#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 铁电·极化📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Anna N. Morozovska, Eugene A. Eliseev, Yujie Zhu, Mykola Yelisieiev, Hanna V. Shevliakova, Yulian M. Vysochanskii, Venkatraman Gopalan, Long-Qing Chen, Jia-Mian Hu🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：挠曲电耦合对电极化和弹性应变涨落的影响，可能导致块体范德瓦耳斯亚铁电体中软光学声子、声学声子和铁子色散关系发生根本性变化。由于尺寸、梯度和应变效应决定着薄膜中的相图与极化行为，因此有理由认为，挠曲电耦合和失配应变应当会强烈影响范德瓦耳斯铁电薄膜中声子和铁子的色散。本文采用朗道–金兹堡–德文希尔方法，揭示了范德瓦耳斯亚铁电体CuInP2S6的薄膜中，源于晶格常数失配的弹性应变的符号和大小会强烈影响软声子和铁子的色散。特别是，当波矢为非零值且满足k>k_cr时，声学声子和铁子的频率趋于零，其中波矢临界值k_cr由失配应变、挠曲电耦合强度和温度共同决定。随着拉伸应变增加，声学声子频率归零，这表明可能出现由挠曲应变效应诱导的空间调制非公度极性相。本文得到的解析结果为范德瓦耳斯亚铁电薄膜中软声子和铁子色散的挠曲应变工程开辟了道路。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22441v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The influence of the flexoelectric coupling on the fluctuations of electric polarization and elastic strains can lead to the principal changes of the dispersion law of soft optical and acoustic phonons and ferrons in a bulk van der Waals ferrielectrics. Since the size, gradient and strain effects determine phase diagrams and polarization behavior in thin films, it is reasonable to assume that the flexocoupling and mismatch strains should have a strong influence on the dispersion of phonons and ferrons in thin films of van der Waals ferroelectrics. Using the Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire approach, in this work we reveal that the dispersion of soft phonons and ferrons is strongly dependent on the sign and magnitude of elastic strains, which originate from the lattice constants mismatch in thin strained films of van der Waals ferrielectric CuInP2S6. In particular, the frequency of acoustic phonons and ferrons tends to zero at nonzero wavevectors k>k_cr, where the critical value of the wavevector k_cr is determined by the mismatch strain, flexoelectric coupling strength and temperature. Zeroing of the acoustic phonon frequency, that appears with increase of tensile strains, indicates a possible emergence of a spatially modulated incommensurate polar phase induced by the flexo-strain effects. Analytical results, derived in this work, open the way for flexo-strain engineering of soft phonon and ferron dispersion in thin films of van der Waals ferrielectrics.

    💡 亮点：挠曲电耦合与晶格失配应变会显著改变范德瓦耳斯亚铁电体薄膜中软光学声子、声学声子和铁子的色散关系。基于朗道–金兹堡–德文希尔理论，研究表明，CuInP2S6薄膜中的声子和铁子色散强烈依赖弹性应变的符号与大小；在一定条件下，有限波矢处的声学模频率趋于零，预示着挠曲应变诱导的空间调制极性相可能形成。该结果为调控范德瓦耳斯亚铁电薄膜中的软模色散提供了理论途径。

    范德瓦耳斯磁体CuCrP₂S₆中连续反铁电和反铁磁有序的微观核磁共振证据#
    Microscopic NMR evidence for successive antiferroelectric and antiferromagnetic order in the van der Waals magnet CuCrP₂S₆#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 C. S. Saramgi, L. F. Prager, S. Selter, Y. Shemerliuk, S. Aswartham, B. Büchner, H. -J. Grafe, K. M. Ranjith🔥 AI 0
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：我们对层状范德瓦耳斯磁体CuCrP₂S₆开展了全面的³¹P和⁶⁵Cu核磁共振（NMR）研究。该化合物表现出一系列结构和磁性相变：高温顺电态，随后在约185 K附近出现准反铁电（QAFE）态，在150 K以下进入长程反铁电（AFE）相，最终在T_N=30 K以下形成反铁磁（AFM）有序。NMR谱、NMR位移以及自旋—晶格（T₁⁻¹）和自旋—自旋（T₂⁻¹）弛豫率的演化，为这些相变提供了直接的微观指纹。AFE相变以下NMR谱线和T₁⁻¹均发生分裂，表明出现了两个不等价的P位点。通过K—χ分析，我们提取出近似各向同性的转移超精细耦合，并表明NMR位移各向异性主要源于偶极贡献，这与Mn₂P₂S₆和Ni₂P₂S₆中的情况不同。我们根据居里—外斯温度确定了铁磁层内交换作用Jᵢₙₜᵣₐ≈−4.9 K，这与沿c轴反铁磁堆叠的铁磁层相一致；同时，我们还评估了包含P—P二聚体交叉关联效应的Moriya高温弛豫率。在T_N附近，T₁⁻¹的临界发散给出了临界指数γ≈0.45(4)，表明CuCrP₂S₆处于三维海森堡普适性区域。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2604.02898v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We present a comprehensive ³¹P and ⁶⁵Cu nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) study of the layered van der Waals magnet CuCrP₂S₆. The compound exhibits a sequence of structural and magnetic phase transitions: a high-temperature paraelectric state, followed by a quasi-antiferroelectric (QAFE) state near 185 K, a long-range antiferroelectric (AFE) phase below 150 K, and finally, antiferromagnetic (AFM) order below T_N = 30 K. The evolution of the NMR spectra, NMR shift, and spin-lattice (T₁⁻¹) and spin-spin (T₂⁻¹) relaxation rates provide direct microscopic fingerprints of these transitions. The splitting of both the NMR line and T₁⁻¹ below the AFE transition demonstrates the emergence of two inequivalent P sites. From K - ḩi analysis, we extract nearly isotropic transferred hyperfine couplings and show that the NMR shift anisotropy originates primarily from the dipolar contribution, in contrast to Mn₂P₂S₆ and Ni₂P₂S₆. We determine the ferromagnetic intralayer exchange Jᵢₙₜᵣₐapprox -4.9 K from the Curie Weiss temperature, consistent with ferromagnetic layers antiferromagnetically stacked along the c axis, and evaluate the Moriya high temperature relaxation rate including cross correlation effects of the P P dimer. Critical divergence of T₁⁻¹ near T_N yields a critical exponent γsimeq 0.45(4), placing CuCrP₂S₆ in a three dimensional Heisenberg universality regime.

    💡 亮点：本研究通过³¹P和⁶⁵Cu核磁共振系统考察层状范德瓦耳斯磁体CuCrP₂S₆中的结构与磁性相变。该材料依次经历高温顺电态、约185 K出现的准反铁电态、150 K以下的长程反铁电相，以及30 K以下的反铁磁有序。核磁共振谱、位移和弛豫率揭示了这些相变的微观特征，并表明该材料处于三维海森堡普适性区域。

    具有铁四极有序的反铁磁拓扑绝缘体候选材料DyPtBi磁性界面上的反常金属态#
    Anomalous metallic states at magnetic interfaces in an antiferromagnetic topological insulator candidate DyPtBi with ferroquadrupolar order#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Kentaro Ueda, Hiraku Saito, Zen Ogame, Isao Ishii, Ruo Hibino, Tatsuya Yanagisawa, Shinichi Itoh, Taro Nakajima, Yoshinori Tokura🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：反铁磁拓扑绝缘体提供了一个富有潜力的平台，在其中，破缺对称性的磁有序与拓扑电子态相互交织。特别是，磁畴壁受到了广泛关注，因为它们像一般磁体中的磁畴一样容易通过外场控制，而且还承载着不同于体相和样品表面中电子态的非平庸电子态。在此，我们报道了一种新的反铁磁拓扑绝缘体候选材料DyPtBi，其中存在可由单轴应力和磁场控制的导电磁畴壁。我们发现，在磁性和结构相变发生时，电阻率会突然增加。与此同时，横向超声模式表现出约6%的显著软化，表明Dy的4f铁四极有序在相变中发挥了重要作用。此外，我们通过中子实验揭示，施加压缩单轴应力能够排列磁畴状态，使电阻率显著增强14%，同时消除导电磁畴壁。这些发现表明，DyPtBi具有与多极自由度纠缠的拓扑电子态，为原位调控拓扑性质提供了一条有前景的途径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22260v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Antiferromagnetic topological insulators provide a fertile platform where symmetry-breaking magnetic order is intertwined with topological electronic states. In particular, magnetic domain walls have attracted much attention, as they can be easily controlled by external fields as in general magnets, and moreover, host nontrivial electronic states distinct from those in bulks and sample surfaces. Here, we report a new antiferromagnetic topological insulator candidate DyPtBi, which hosts conductive magnetic domain walls controllable by uniaxial stress and magnetic field. We find that the resistivity exhibits abrupt increase upon the magnetic and structural phase transition. Concomitantly, the transverse ultrasonic mode shows remarkable softening of 6 %, indicating that the Dy 4f ferroquadrupolar order plays a vital role in the phase transition. Furthermore, we reveal by neutron experiments that applying compressive uniaxial stress aligns the magnetic domain state, leading to the strong resistivity enhancement of 14 % while eliminating the conductive magnetic domain walls. These findings demonstrate that DyPtBi exhibits topological electronic states entangled with multipolar degrees of freedom, providing a promising route for in situ control of topological properties.

    💡 亮点：本文报道了反铁磁拓扑绝缘体候选材料DyPtBi，并发现其中存在可由单轴应力和磁场控制的导电磁畴壁。磁性和结构相变伴随电阻率突增以及横向超声模式约6%的显著软化，表明Dy的4f铁四极有序在相变中发挥重要作用。中子实验显示，压缩单轴应力能够排列磁畴并消除导电磁畴壁，使电阻率增强14%，揭示了多极自由度与拓扑电子态的耦合及其原位调控潜力。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到HfO₂、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂、二维范德华铁电、钙钛矿氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    一维铁磁体的动态磁对密度函数#
    Dynamic Magnetic Pair-Density Function of a One-Dimensional Ferromagnet#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Shin-ichi Shamoto🔥 AI 87
    相关度
    5

    📄 摘要：通过将静态磁对分布函数（mPDF）扩展到有限能量转移，可以推导出动态磁对密度函数（DymPDF）Dₘ₍ᵣ,E)。我们从磁振子色散关系出发，获得了一维 Heisenberg 铁磁体的 DymPDF 解析表达式，并将其与使用 SpinW 进行的模拟进行比较。对于最近邻自旋对关联的能量依赖性，两者达到了极好的一致，表明 DymPDF 会在磁振子模式转变处改变符号，而该转变发生在最大磁振子能量的一半处。通过采用包含有限仪器分辨率、磁关联长度和傅里叶截断效应的模型，低能量下的实空间 DymPDF 也得到了再现。这些结果为利用 DymPDF 分析研究磁性材料中的局域自旋动力学建立了理论基础。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21759v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The dynamic magnetic pair-density function (DymPDF) Dₘ̊ M(r, E) is derived by extending the static magnetic pair distribution function (mPDF) to finite energy transfer. The analytical DymPDF of a one-dimensional Heisenberg ferromagnet is obtained from the magnon dispersion and compared with simulations performed using textsc SpinW. Excellent agreement is achieved for energy dependence of the nearest-neighbor spin-pair correlation, demonstrating that the DymPDF changes sign at the magnon-mode transition occurring at one-half of the maximum magnon energy. The real-space DymPDF at low energy is also reproduced with a model including finite instrumental resolution, magnetic correlation length, and Fourier-termination effects. These results establish the theoretical foundation of DymPDF analysis for investigating local spin dynamics in magnetic materials.

    💡 亮点：本文将静态磁对分布函数扩展到有限能量转移，定义了动态磁对密度函数 DymPDF。作者从磁振子色散关系推导了一维 Heisenberg 铁磁体的解析 DymPDF，并与 SpinW 模拟进行了比较。结果表明，最近邻自旋对关联的能量依赖关系吻合良好，DymPDF 会在磁振子模态转变处改变符号；考虑仪器分辨率、磁关联长度和傅里叶截断后，低能实空间结果也能得到再现。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    Enhancement of superconductivity by polarization of magnetic impurities in disordered films#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat.supr-con+cond-mat.mtrl-sci+cond-mat.str-el+physics.comp-ph+physics.chem-ph👤 Gleb S. Seleznev, Yakov V. Fominov

    📄 摘要：含磁性杂质的脏超导薄膜在磁场使杂质自旋极化时，可能表现出非平凡行为。正如Kharitonov和Feigelman（KF）所预测的那样，这种极化会降低交换散射率。因此，当磁场引起的破对效应较弱时，平行磁场能够提高临界温度T_c；强自旋轨道散射和较小膜厚的情况正可以实现这一条件。近期，Llanos等人观察到显著的T_c增强，这与KF理论一致。同一实验还报道了平行磁场使垂直上临界场H_c2⊥增强，并使伦敦穿透深度λ_L减小。这些量在原始KF理论中并未被考虑。为弥补这一缺口，我们基于脏超导体的Gor'kov图示技术建立了一个理论框架。我们沿着两个与实验相关的方向扩展KF理论：（i）推广到任意温度T

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2605.17408v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Dirty superconducting films with magnetic impurities can exhibit nontrivial behavior in a magnetic field that polarizes the impurity spins. As predicted by Kharitonov and Feigelman (KF) [JETP Lett. 82, 421 (2005)], this polarization reduces the exchange scattering rate. Consequently, a parallel magnetic field can enhance the critical temperature T_c when magnetic-field pair breaking is weak, as realized for strong spin-orbit scattering and small film thickness. Recently, Llanos et al. [Nat. Phys. 22, 856 (2026)] observed a pronounced enhancement of T_c consistent with the KF theory. The same experiment also reported an enhancement of the perpendicular upper critical field Hc₂perp and a suppression of the London penetration depth łambda_L by a parallel magnetic field. These quantities were not considered in the original KF theory. To address this gap, we develop a theoretical framework based on Gor'kov's diagrammatic technique for dirty superconductors. We extend the KF theory in two experimentally relevant directions: (i) to arbitrary temperatures T

    💡 亮点：含磁性杂质的脏超导薄膜在磁场极化杂质自旋时可能表现出非平凡行为。根据Kharitonov和Feigelman理论，杂质极化会降低交换散射率，因此在磁场导致的破对效应较弱时，平行磁场能够提高临界温度。本文基于Gor'kov图示技术建立理论框架，进一步研究平行磁场对垂直上临界场和伦敦穿透深度的影响；所给摘要在“任意温度”处截断，具体后续内容需查阅原文。

    文献研究：Machine Learning to Foundation Models: Artificial Intelligence for Nanophotonic Modeling and Scientific Discovery#
    Machine Learning to Foundation Models: Artificial Intelligence for Nanophotonic Modeling and Scientific Discovery#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Chaobin Yang, Xueqing Liu, Yiqun Fu, Fengbo Zhou, Krzysztof Kempa, Stefano Anzellotti, Michael J. Naughton🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    3
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21612v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to model, design, and study nanophotonic systems. This review traces the development of the field from classical machine learning and deep learning to generative models, transfer learning, transformers, and emerging foundation models. It first introduces major nanophotonic platforms, including nanoparticles, nanoholes, metasurfaces, photonic crystals, multilayer thin films, and integrated photonic devices, together with their main forward and inverse problems. It then reviews data-driven methods for predicting optical spectra and fields, generating structures from target responses, improving designs through optimization, and accounting for fabrication constraints. Generative models are discussed as a way to produce multiple valid solutions to nonunique inverse problems, while transfer learning, few-shot learning, and physics-aware training help reduce data requirements and improve generalization. Recent domain-specific foundation models show that different optical structures and responses can be handled within shared representations, but current systems remain limited in scope and physical grounding. Future progress will depend on multimodal models that connect geometry, materials, spectra, electromagnetic (EM) fields, fabrication data, experiments, and scientific literature with reliable simulation and validation tools. Current foundation models remain domain-specific, and their extension to broader nanophotonic tasks will require stronger physical grounding and validation.

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23141v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Cosmic muon tomography is a powerful non-destructive imaging technique for inspecting dense and shielded materials through multiple Coulomb scattering. In this work, we present the design, simulation, and performance evaluation of a complete muon tomography system comprising six scintillator-strip tracking stations for trajectory reconstruction and a four-station magnetic spectrometer for muon momentum estimation. The detector geometry is implemented in the GEANT4 framework and optimized for object localization and material characterization. The reconstructed momentum is combined with the scattering angle to define the scattering density h̊oₛ = (θ p)²/Lₑff, which enhances sensitivity to material-dependent scattering. Point-of-Closest-Approach (PoCA) reconstruction is used to estimate scattering locations within the imaging volume. To detect and separate multiple unknown objects, Hierarchical Density-Based Spatial Clustering of Applications with Noise (HDBSCAN) is applied to the reconstructed PoCA cloud. Cluster-level scattering and geometric features are then extracted for object characterization. The proposed framework enables object detection, localization, volume estimation, shape reconstruction, and material ranking within a unified analysis pipeline. Simulation studies with multiple objects of different compositions demonstrate accurate reconstruction of object positions and geometries, while providing reliable material discrimination based on scattering density. The developed system offers a scalable approach for next-generation cosmic muon tomography applications in security screening, nuclear waste characterization, and non-destructive inspection.

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2608.23141v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Cosmic muon tomography is a powerful non-destructive imaging technique for inspecting dense and shielded materials through multiple Coulomb scattering.…

    单斜TbFe₂D₄.₂氘化物亚铁磁结构的确定及其随温度和高磁场的演化#
    Determination of the ferrimagnetic structure of monoclinic TbFe₂D₄.₂ deuteride and its evolution versus temperature and high magnetic field#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Valerie Paul-Boncour, Olivier Isnard🔥 AI 72
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：通过结合不同的磁性测量（包括高脉冲磁场测量）以及随温度和外加磁场变化的中子衍射实验，研究了单斜TbFe₂D₄.₂的磁性质。TbFe₂D₄.₂具有亚铁磁基态，其中Tb磁矩MTb = 8.2(2) μB，Fe磁矩MFe = 2.1(2) μB。在4.2 K时观察到与Tb磁矩有关的强磁各向异性，因为即使达到50 T也尚未实现饱和。此外，在3 T和29 T的磁场下观察到两个变磁转变。升温时，Tb磁矩逐渐减小，而Fe磁矩在150 K之前保持不变。在160 K时，Tb和Fe磁矩均突然减小，同时晶胞体积收缩了0.38%，这归因于Fe子晶格中巡游电子的变磁行为。由于晶胞体积增大以及Fe磁矩与Tb磁矩之间的交换相互作用，该转变温度比YFe₂D₄.₂的转变温度高76 K。该氘化物在220 K以上转变为顺磁态。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23346v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The magnetic properties of monoclinic TbFe₂D₄.₂ have been investigated by combining different magnetic measurements, including those involving a high pulsed magnetic field, with neutron diffraction experiments versus temperature and applied field. TbFe₂D₄.₂ displays a ferrimagnetic ground state with MTb = 8.2(2) μB and MFₑ = 2.1(2) μB. At 4.2 K a large magnetic anisotropy related to the Tb moments is observed, as saturation is not reached at 50 T. In addition, two metamagnetic transitions are observed for fields of 3 and 29 T. Upon heating, the Tb moment decreases progressively whereas the Fe moment remains constant up to 150 K. At 160 K both Tb and Fe moments sharply decrease, and a 0.38% contraction of the cell volume is observed due to an itinerant electron metamagnetic behavior of the Fe sublattice. This transition temperature is 76 K higher than that of YFe₂D₄.₂ due to an increase in cell volume, as well as exchange interactions between the Fe and Tb moments. The deuteride becomes paramagnetic above 220 K.

    💡 亮点：本文结合多种磁性测量、高脉冲磁场实验以及随温度和外场变化的中子衍射，研究了单斜TbFe₂D₄.₂的磁性。该化合物基态为亚铁磁态，Tb和Fe磁矩分别为8.2(2) μB和2.1(2) μB；在4.2 K时表现出与Tb磁矩有关的强磁各向异性，并在3 T和29 T处出现两个变磁转变。升温过程中，Tb磁矩逐渐减小而Fe磁矩保持不变至150 K，160 K时二者均显著下降并伴随晶胞体积收缩，氘化物在220 K以上转变为顺磁态。

    双层铱酸盐Sr₃Ir₂O₇中应变驱动的自旋翻转转变与巨磁振子能隙塌缩#
    Strain-driven spin-flop transition and collapse of the giant magnon gap in the bilayer iridate Sr₃Ir₂O₇#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Choong H. Kim🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：双层铱酸盐Sr₃Ir₂O₇是一种c轴共线反铁磁体，其这一磁结构由巨大的层间赝偶极各向异性维持，而单层材料Sr₂IrO₄的自旋则在ab平面内发生倾斜。我们通过第一性原理计算表明，几个百分点的双轴压缩应变（ε_c≈−2.4%）会使Sr₃Ir₂O₇的易磁化轴翻转到面内。我们利用Wannier函数构建了一个不含拟合参数的磁性模型哈密顿量；该模型能够再现块体材料的巨大磁振子能隙，而这一能隙此前只能通过实验拟合得到，并由此确定了相应机制。压缩应变会使层间交换通道塌缩，因为直线型Ir–O–Ir路径变弱，而弯曲的面内路径增强。Hund交换决定了各向异性的尺度，并且当J/U超过约0.15时，会彻底消除共线态。该翻转并非刚性旋转，因为两种状态中的有序磁矩会在ε_c处相交；它还具有鲜明特征，即巨大的易轴磁振子能隙会塌缩为无能隙的、类似Goldstone模的谱。因此，受压应变的薄膜处在一条以零温双临界点为终点的变磁相边界上，为调节磁矩方向提供了一种电荷中性的控制手段。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22473v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The bilayer iridate Sr₃Ir₂O₇ is a c-axis collinear antiferromagnet, held there by a giant interlayer pseudodipolar anisotropy, whereas single-layer Sr₂IrO₄ cants in the ab plane. We show from first principles that biaxial compression of a few percent (ǎrepsilon_capprox-2.4%) flops the easy axis of Sr₃Ir₂O₇ into the plane. A magnetic model Hamiltonian built from Wannier functions with no fitted parameter---reproducing the giant magnon gap of the bulk, so far known only from fits to experiment---identifies the mechanism. Compression collapses the interlayer exchange channel, whose straight Ir--O--Ir path weakens as the bent in-plane path strengthens. Hund's exchange sets the scale of the anisotropy and, beyond J/Uapprox0.15, removes the collinear state altogether. The flop is not a rigid rotation---the ordered moments of the two states cross at ǎrepsilon_c---and it carries a stark fingerprint, in that the giant easy-axis magnon gap collapses to a gapless, Goldstone-like spectrum. Compressively strained films thus sit on a metamagnetic phase boundary ending in a zero-temperature bicritical point, a charge-neutral handle on the moment direction.

    💡 亮点：第一性原理计算表明，几个百分点的双轴压缩应变可使双层铱酸盐Sr₃Ir₂O₇的易磁化轴从c轴翻转至面内方向。其机制在于层间交换通道减弱，而面内交换通道增强；Hund交换决定了各向异性的能标，并在较大J/U下消除共线反铁磁态。该自旋翻转伴随着巨大的易轴磁振子能隙塌缩为近似无能隙的Goldstone型谱，说明受压薄膜可处于亚铁磁相变边界。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：有效哈密顿量或机器学习哈密顿量, Wannier 插值和有效电子模型，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

  17. 58
    通过动态磁性对密度函数分析研究局域磁振子模式#
    Local magnon modes studied by dynamic magnetic pair-density function analysis#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat.supr-con+cond-mat.mtrl-sci+cond-mat.str-el+physics.comp-ph+physics.chem-ph👤 Shin-ichi Shamoto, Yukio Yasui, Kazuki Iida, Yasuhiro Inamura, Motoyuki Ishikado, Mitsutaka Nakamura, Lieh-Jeng Chang
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：动态磁性对密度函数DₘM(r,E)是通过对动态磁结构因子SₘM(Q,E)进行傅里叶变换得到的，而SₘM(Q,E)则利用非极化非弹性中子散射测量。尽管利用SₘM研究磁激发已有很长历史，但此前尚无DₘM(r,E)的相关报道。在本研究中，我们考察了简单磁性模型以及FeTiO₃和YBa₂Cu₃O₆等代表性磁体，以研究DₘM(r,E)在实空间中的动力学。我们在低能极限下推导了简单磁性模型的DₘM(r,E)方程。通过将这些方程与模拟结果进行比较，我们展示了实空间局域磁振子模式所具有的特征能量依赖，包括磁振子模式从声学型向光学型的转变。我们的新型分析揭示，即使在非周期条件下，纳米尺度实空间中的局域磁振子模式也会伴随特定能量下每个自旋对关联的符号变化。该方法为研究局域磁动力学提供了独特手段。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2606.31655v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: The dynamic magnetic pair-density function (DymPDF) Dₘ̊ M(r, E) is obtained via the Fourier transform of the dynamic magnetic structure factor, Sₘ̊ M(Q, E), which is measured using nonpolarized inelastic neutron scattering. While there is a long history of magnetic excitation studies with Sₘ̊ M(Q, E), there are no reports on Dₘ̊ M(r, E). In this study, we examine simple magnet models and representative magnet examples, such as FeTiO₃ and YBa₂Cu₃O₆, to investigate the real-space dynamics of Dₘ̊ M(r, E). We derive the Dₘ̊ M(r, E) equations for simple magnet models in a low energy limit. By comparing these equations to the simulations, we demonstrate the characteristic energy dependence of real-space local magnon modes, including the transition of the magnon mode from acoustic to optical. Our novel analysis reveals the local magnon modes accompanied by a sign change in each spin-pair correlation at a given energy in nanoscale real space even under non-periodic conditions. This method is unique for studying local magnetic dynamics.

    💡 亮点：动态磁性对密度函数DₘM(r,E)由非极化非弹性中子散射测得的动态磁结构因子SₘM(Q,E)傅里叶变换得到，但此前尚无相关报道。本文通过简单磁性模型以及FeTiO₃和YBa₂Cu₃O₆等代表性磁体，研究DₘM(r,E)在实空间中的动力学。结果展示了局域磁振子模式的特征能量依赖，包括磁振子模式从声学型向光学型的转变，并揭示了即使在非周期条件下，纳米尺度实空间中自旋对关联也会随能量发生符号变化。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：State-Space Model-Enabled Reinforcement Learning for Magnetic Configuration Controlon EXL-50U#
    State-Space Model-Enabled Reinforcement Learning for Magnetic Configuration Controlon EXL-50U#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Pei Guo, Zhengyuan Chen, Jianguo Chen, Xuanhe Wang, Guoyang Shi, Siqi Ding, Yapeng Zhang, Lei Xing, Yong Liu, Xiang Gu, Tiantian Sun, Xiuchun Lun, Jia Li, Zhengxiong Wang, Huasheng Xie, Hanyue Zhao, Yuejiang Shi, Xianming Song, Tianyuan Liu, EXL-50U Team🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    7
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.21737v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Accurate feedback control of the plasma current (Iₚ₎ and centroid position (R_c,Z_c) is essential for the stable operation of spherical torus (ST) plasmas. Conventional proportional-integral-derivative (PID) controllers require extensive manual tuning and struggle with the fast, strongly coupled dynamics that arise as plasma performance improves. Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a promising alternative to such complex magnetic control problems, yet its practical deployment on ST devices remains challenging. This paper presents a practical RL controller for the EXL-50U ST, trained within a rigid RZIP state-space model (SSM) that enables efficient offline policy learning. A lightweight plasma position reconstructor is developed to estimate (R_c,Z_c) from magnetic probe signals within the real-time control cycle. The trained policy is seamlessly deployed on the EXL-50U plasma control system, achieving stable regulation of Iₚ and (R_c,Z_c) and sustaining discharges up to 650 ms under RL control. These results demonstrate the feasibility and practical potential of model-informed RL for magnetic configuration control in ST devices, offering a promising direction beyond conventional PID-based schemes.

    基于神经算子的太阳内部边界多场状态重构#
    Neural Operator based Multi-Field Reconstruction of Inner Solar Boundary State#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Vignesh Kumar Pandian Sathia, Reza Mansouri, Dustin J. Kempton, Pete Riley, Rafal A. Angryk🔥 AI 100
    相关度
    0

    📄 摘要：太阳风是从太阳表面不断喷出的带电粒子流，其运动受复杂且相互作用的磁流体动力学过程支配。准确规定内部边界条件对于日球层建模和太阳风预测至关重要。在许多实际应用中，能够直接获得的只是相互作用多场变量中的一部分，但为了全面开展太阳风预测和后续磁流体动力学模拟，还需要更加完整的边界状态。在本研究中，我们利用算子学习研究了在30个太阳半径处学习太阳磁流体动力学多场、多尺度状态的问题。具体而言，给定径向速度和径向磁场，我们旨在重构非径向速度和磁场分量、径向和非径向电流密度、热力学密度，以及压力分量。由于该映射具有高度非线性、空间耦合和多尺度特征，因此对数据驱动的科学机器学习而言是一个具有挑战性的任务。为解决这一问题，我们采用局部神经算子（Local Neural Operator，LocalNO），在学习输入函数空间到输出函数空间映射的同时，保留局部性和分辨率感知能力。与传统回归模型和自编码器模型不同，神经算子更适合学习物理系统中产生的结构化场到场变换。最终得到的预测结果及其输入变量，旨在作为未来日球内部建模流程的边界条件变量。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.22782v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The Solar wind is a continuous flow of charged particles emanating from the solar surface and governed by complex, interacting magnetohydrodynamic processes. Accurate specification of inner-boundary conditions is essential for heliospheric modeling and solar-wind prediction. In many practical applications, only a subset of interacting multi-field variables is directly available, but for a comprehensive view of solar wind prediction and downstream magnetohydrodynamic simulations, a more complete boundary state is required. In this work, we study the problem of learning the multi-field multi-scale solar magnetohydrodynamic state at 30 solar radii (R_ødot) using operator learning. Specifically, given the radial velocity and radial magnetic field, we aim to reconstruct the non-radial velocity and magnetic field components, radial and non-radial current density, thermodynamic density, and pressure components. This mapping is highly nonlinear, spatially coupled, and multi-scale, making it a challenging task for data-driven scientific machine learning. To address this problem, we employ a Local Neural Operator (LocalNO) that learns mappings between input and output function spaces while retaining locality and resolution-awareness. Unlike conventional regression models and autoencoder models, neural operators are better suited for learning structured field-to-field transformations arising from physical systems. The resulting predictions along with inputs are intended to serve as boundary condition variables for future inner-heliospheric modeling pipelines.

    💡 亮点：本文研究利用算子学习，在30个太阳半径处重构太阳磁流体动力学多场、多尺度边界状态的问题。给定径向速度和径向磁场，模型旨在预测非径向速度和磁场分量、径向及非径向电流密度，以及热力学密度和压力分量。作者采用局部神经算子LocalNO，以保持局部性和分辨率感知能力；所得预测结果可与输入变量共同用于未来日球内部建模流程的边界条件设定。
